          (16): BatchNorm2d(256, eps=0.001, momentum=0.01, affine=True, track_running_stats=True)

        )
      )
    )
    init_cfg={'type': 'Kaiming', 'layer': 'Conv2d'}
    (neck): SECONDFPN(

          (0): Conv2d(128, 256, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False)

        )
      )

  )
  (heads): ModuleDict(
